        function waitForPageLoad(callback) {
            if (document.readyState == 'complete') {
                console.log('ready state complete');
                return callback();
            } else {
                console.log('else');
                if (document.readyState !== 'complete') {
                    console.log('ready state not complete');
                    return setTimeout(waitForPageLoad.bind(null, callback), 30);
                }
            }
        }

        function addEventListeners() {
            window.clickMetricToggle = false;

            SS.SPA.dynamicModify({
                "staticContainer": "body",

                    if(clubElement.classList.contains('ssChecked') === false){

<script defer="">
    (function () {

        var sitespect_url = location.pathname + location.search;

        function checkUrl(sitespect_url) {
            var clubPartialRegex = /\/club\/[0-9]+(?:$|\?.*)/gi;
            var clubFullRegex = /\/club\/[^/]+\/[0-9]+(?:$|\?.*)/gi;
            var matchesClubRegex = clubPartialRegex.test(sitespect_url);

            if(matchesClubRegex !== true){
                matchesClubRegex = clubFullRegex.test(sitespect_url);
            }

            if(matchesClubRegex === true){
                var randomValue = Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000) + 1;
                var xhttp = new XMLHttpRequest();
                xhttp.open("POST", "/__ssobj/track?event=clubPageViewedV2&value=undefined&x=" + randomValue, true);
                xhttp.setRequestHeader("Content-type", "application/x-www-form-urlencoded");
                xhttp.send();
            }
        }

        var checkIfUrlChanged = function () {
            setTimeout(function () {

                // If the URL has changed
                if (sitespect_url !== location.pathname + location.search) {

                    // Set the new URL as current
                    sitespect_url = location.pathname + location.search;

                    // Print the New URL
                    console.log("New Page: " + sitespect_url);

                    checkUrl(sitespect_url);

                }
            }, 750);
        };

        document.body.addEventListener('click', function () {
            checkIfUrlChanged();
        });

        window.addEventListener('popstate', checkIfUrlChanged);

        checkUrl(sitespect_url);

    }());
</script>
<script defer="">
    window.SS && SS.Require(function () {

        function waitForPageLoad(callback) {
            if (document.readyState == 'complete') {
                console.log('ready state complete');
                return callback();
            } else {
                console.log('else');
                if (document.readyState !== 'complete') {
                    console.log('ready state not complete');
                    return setTimeout(waitForPageLoad.bind(null, callback), 30);
                }
            }
        }

        function addEventListeners() {
            window.clickMetricToggle = false;

            // Login Page Viewed and click metrics on page
            SS.SPA.dynamicModify({
                "staticContainer": "body",
                "dynamicContainer": "div.sc-auth-page.sc-login",
                "callbackFunction": function () {

                    let loginPage = document.querySelector('div.sc-auth-page.sc-login');
                    if(loginPage !== null && loginPage !== undefined){
                        if(loginPage.classList.contains('ssChecked') === false) {
                            loginPage.classList.add('ssChecked');
                            SS.EventTrack.rp('loginPageViewed');
                            let forgotEmailLink = document.querySelector('#main > div > div:nth-child(1) > div.sc-login-forgot-links > a:nth-child(1)');
                            let forgotPasswordLink = document.querySelector('#main > div > div:nth-child(1) > div.sc-login-forgot-links > a:nth-child(2)');
                            let checkBoxSignIn = document.querySelector('#main > div > div:nth-child(1) > label > div > input');
                            let joinNowLink = document.querySelector('#main > div > div:nth-child(3) > div:nth-child(2) > a');
                            let chatWithUsLink = document.querySelector('#main > div > div:nth-child(3) > div:nth-child(3) > a');
                            let signInButton = document.querySelector('#main > div > div:nth-child(1) > form > button');
                            let registerYourMembership = document.querySelector('#main > div > div:nth-child(3) > div:nth-child(1) > button');
                            if(forgotEmailLink !== null && forgotEmailLink !== undefined) {
                                forgotEmailLink.addEventListener('click', function () {
                                    SS.EventTrack.rp('forgotEmailLinkClicked');
                                });
                            }
                            if(forgotPasswordLink !== null && forgotPasswordLink !== undefined) {
                                forgotPasswordLink.addEventListener('click', function () {
                                    SS.EventTrack.rp('forgotPasswordLinkClicked');
                                });
                            }
                            if(checkBoxSignIn !== null && checkBoxSignIn !== undefined) {
                                checkBoxSignIn.addEventListener('change', function () {
                                    SS.EventTrack.rp('checkBoxSignInClicked');
                                });
                            }
                            if(joinNowLink !== null && joinNowLink !== undefined) {
                                joinNowLink.addEventListener('click', function () {
                                    SS.EventTrack.rp('joinNowLinkClicked');
                                });
                            }
                            if(chatWithUsLink !== null && chatWithUsLink !== undefined) {
                                chatWithUsLink.addEventListener('click', function () {
                                    SS.EventTrack.rp('chatWithUsLinkClicked');
                                });
                            }
                            if(signInButton !== null && signInButton !== undefined) {
                                signInButton.addEventListener('click', function () {
                                    SS.EventTrack.rp('signInButtonClicked');
                                });
                            }
                            if(registerYourMembership !== null && registerYourMembership !== undefined) {
                                registerYourMembership.addEventListener('click', function () {
                                    SS.EventTrack.rp('registerYourMembershipClicked');
                                });
                            }
                        }
                    }

                },
                "runCallbackNow": false,
                "watchSubtree": true
            });
            // Forgot password Page Viewed
            SS.SPA.dynamicModify({
                "staticContainer": "body",
                "dynamicContainer": "div.sc-forgot-password-page",
                "callbackFunction": function () {

                    let forgotPasswordPage = document.querySelector('div.sc-forgot-password-page');
                    if(forgotPasswordPage !== null && forgotPasswordPage !== undefined) {
                        if (forgotPasswordPage.classList.contains('ssChecked') === false) {
                            forgotPasswordPage.classList.add('ssChecked');
                            SS.EventTrack.rp('forgotPasswordPageViewed');
                        }
                    }

                },
                "runCallbackNow": false,
                "watchSubtree": true
            });
            // Forgot email Page Viewed
            SS.SPA.dynamicModify({
                "staticContainer": "body",
                "dynamicContainer": "div.sc-forgot-email-page",
                "callbackFunction": function () {

                    let forgotEmailPage = document.querySelector('div.sc-forgot-email-page');
                    if(forgotEmailPage !== null && forgotEmailPage !== undefined) {
                        if (forgotEmailPage.classList.contains('ssChecked') === false) {
                            forgotEmailPage.classList.add('ssChecked');
                            SS.EventTrack.rp('forgotEmailPageViewed');
                        }
                    }

                },
                "runCallbackNow": false,
                "watchSubtree": true
            });

            // register Membership Page Viewed
            SS.SPA.dynamicModify({
                "staticContainer": "body",
                "dynamicContainer": "#main > div.sc-auth-page.sc-registration-page",
                "callbackFunction": function () {

                    let forgotEmailPage = document.querySelector('#main > div.sc-auth-page.sc-registration-page');
                    if(forgotEmailPage !== null && forgotEmailPage !== undefined) {
                        if (forgotEmailPage.classList.contains('ssChecked') === false) {

                            SS.EventTrack.rp('registerMembershipPageViewed');
                        }
                    }

                "staticContainer": "body",
                "dynamicContainer": "#main > div.sc-registration-password-form.sc-auth-form",
                "callbackFunction": function () {
